To find the total number of balloons, we need to sum the quantities of each color of balloon present in the classroom.

1.  **Identify the given quantities:**
    *   Red balloons: 10
    *   Blue balloons: 4
    *   Green balloons: 12

2.  **Formulate the calculation:** The total number of balloons is the sum of the red, blue, and green balloons.
    Total balloons = Number of red balloons + Number of blue balloons + Number of green balloons
    Total balloons = 10 + 4 + 12

3.  **Perform the addition:**
    *   First, add the red and blue balloons: 10 + 4 = 14
    *   Next, add the green balloons to this subtotal: 14 + 12 = 26

Therefore, there are 26 balloons in total.

To determine the total number of coins collected by the player, a summation operation is performed on the quantities of each type of coin. The player collected 9 bronze coins, 8 silver coins, and 5 gold coins.

**Step-by-step rationale:**
1.  Identify the number of bronze coins: 9.
2.  Identify the number of silver coins: 8.
3.  Identify the number of gold coins: 5.
4.  To calculate the total, sum these individual quantities: Total Coins = Bronze Coins + Silver Coins + Gold Coins.
5.  Perform the addition: 9 + 8 + 5.
6.  First, add the bronze and silver coins: 9 + 8 = 17.
7.  Next, add the gold coins to this subtotal: 17 + 5 = 22.

Thus, the player has collected a total of 22 coins.

- Number of squares = 1
- Number of circles = 6

- Total shapes = 8

Therefore, the total number of shapes is 8.
